# vSAN Metrics (API)

Generated by `tools/build.py` from [raw/catalog.json](raw/catalog.json), [raw/schema.json](raw/schema.json) and [raw/live.json](raw/live.json); do not edit by hand.

PERF and VCPERF rows were listed live by the vSAN services of a VCF 9.1 vCenter; PERF rows marked *(live only)* returned values but are missing from that list. VSAN rows come from the API type definitions; those marked *(schema)* were not seen with a value on the live cluster. Notes give the tier (advanced, verbose, hotspot; blank = default) and the description the API returns. Raw host-internal counters are in [internal-api.md](internal-api.md).

Live evidence from `tools/live.py` (2026-10-19): vCenter and ESX 9.1.1, one OSA vSAN cluster with 3 hosts. Not enabled on that cluster, so their fields could not be seen: dedup, encryption, fileService, iscsi, stretchedOr2Node, perfVerboseMode.

**Source legend**

| Code | API |
|---|---|
| PERF | vSAN performance service: `VsanPerformanceManager.VsanPerfQueryPerf` with entity `<entity type>:<id>` and the metric label. Entity types and metrics listed live by `VsanPerfGetSupportedEntityTypes` |
| VSAN | vSAN Management API query methods on vCenter (`https://<vcenter>/vsanHealth`, SOAP). Field paths from the pyVmomi 9.1 type definitions |
| VCPERF | vCenter `PerformanceManager` counters in a vSAN group (vim25 API) |
| VIM | vSAN properties of vCenter inventory objects (vim25 API): `ClusterComputeResource`, `HostSystem`, `Datastore` |

**Live column**: ✓ = returned with a value on a live vSAN cluster; *no data* = the entity type answered but this metric
had no values; *not run* = health check absent from the cluster's health summary; blank = not seen live (see *(schema)*).

## Live coverage by performance entity type

How each entity type answered `VsanPerfQueryPerf` on the live cluster. *ID format* is the entity ID that returned values (`*` = wildcard accepted). Entity types with no data belong to features that are not enabled on that cluster (ESA, deduplication, file service, iSCSI, vSAN Direct, PMem, remote datastores, data protection), or need verbose mode.
